# GSQ224 owned vision and full-context functional acceptance

The new source admits only the exact existing GSQ224 overlay into an explicit grouped vision profile. It retains the original tower and metadata ownership, complete tower/workspace accounting, fixed context, sequential replacement contract and original draft. Image and lookahead combination is still refused. The measured text recipe and public registry/Auto remain unchanged. The context diagnostic now records the actual artifact's arithmetic tag, rather than incorrectly labeling every affine artifact as three-bit.

All 104 existing T0/T1 catalogue groups pass. The real owned-vision instrument then passes all 49 assertions, including corrupt/deleted metadata, cancellation, bounded workspace, unchanged tower features, drafted/plain token equality, retained continuation and actual HTTP handlers. Its physical peak is 6,785,160,232 bytes. It does not establish image-answer accuracy.

The existing full-context instrument subsequently consumes 32,768 tokens and passes all 1,445 assertions. It covers complete target/draft state, every recorded verification prefix, rollback, continuation and refusal before mutating a full context. Physical peak is 7,671,123,808 bytes. The direct-model fixture has 640 slots and a 128-MB allocator cache. This is functional and physical evidence under the ten-GB watchdog, not a planner-slack calculation or full Engine long-context speed result. Original guards, real headroom and single-process ownership remain intact. The source-bound executable and archive hashes are preserved below.

All four CI workflows for the prior measured source a53ca0c39ee9288d67de273f5303dfbbb5916903 have completed successfully. They do not substitute for final checks on this new feature source.

A separate future component hypothesis is frozen, not executed at capture: QLinear's exact small-row mode creates a quantized projection per token, whereas the pinned MLX backend can retain M=1 in a batch dimension. The existing six-shape component instrument will first require finite bit-exact equality, then test cost with rotating banks. Ordinary variable-row batching remains rejected; no target arithmetic or source is changed for this hypothesis. A mismatch stops this construction. This is one prospective dispatch experiment, not another quantization framework.

One superseded generated executable is retired only after verifying its identity and complete retained source archive. Original weights, installations, all unique model payloads, accepted builds and raw failures remain.

## Final local source acceptance

The complete static suite passes on the unchanged source-bound feature executable, including all planner, independent memory-override, transport, installer, source-policy and brain gates. The source archive, executable, library and every current implementation source still authenticate after acceptance. This validates retained research capability and original safety behavior; the separate image instruction-following failure still prohibits GSQ224 product admission. Exact committed-source CI is separate.
